THIRD-PARTY NOTICES
BUILD SCOPE
Linux amd64; CGO=0; `services/file-processor/cmd/file-processor`, plus separately rebuilt `grpc-health-probe@v0.4.57` (9 external module versions; Dockerfile-pinned go get set); union of both executable closures.

These notices accompany the listed Go executable(s). The repository first-party LICENSE is a separate file. Each upstream license/notice body below is preserved from its observed source file.

RIGHTS REVIEW — NEXUS MODULE
The pinned `github.com/nexus-rpc/nexus-proto-annotations@v0.1.0` tag commit `e558d6e` (2026-03-30) contains no LICENSE. Upstream commit `835bd8d49cb45c8efa22614164b90335f7e56918` (2026-06-29) adds the MIT license below. The tag README, proto, and generated Go files have the same blob IDs as that later commit; the complete tree comparison shows only `A LICENSE`. This preserves the later upstream notice and source-grant chronology. Whether the later grant reaches code in the earlier v0.1.0 tag remains for owner/rightsholder review before distribution.
